
============================================================
COMPUTING METRICS
============================================================

--- CBR-CDB ---
give me the number of patients who had single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass.
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE PROCEDURES."LONG_TITLE" = "Single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['procedures."long_title"', '=', 'single internal mammary-coronary artery bypass']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PROCEDURES on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PROCEDURES.HADM_ID WHERE PROCEDURES."SHORT_TITLE" = "1 int mam-cor art bypass"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'procedures'], 'cond': [['procedures."short_title"', '=', '1 int mam-cor art bypass']]}
----------

----------

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'diphenhydramine hcl']]}
----------
TRUTH:

Calculate the number of patients admitted to emergency who had an ld body fluid test by lab
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "EMERGENCY" AND LAB."FLUID" = "BLOOD"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['lab."fluid"', '=', 'blood']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."ADMISSION_TYPE" = "EMERGENCY" AND LAB."LABEL" = "LD, Body Fluid"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."admission_type"', '=', 'emergency'], ['lab."label"', '=', 'ld, body fluid']]}
----------

PREDS:

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions', 'lab'], 'cond': [['lab."label"', '=', 'sodium, urine'], ['prescriptions."drug_type"', '=', 'base']]}

{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions', 'lab'], 'cond': [['lab."label"', '=', 'sodium, urine'], ['prescriptions."drug_type"', '=', 'main']]}

----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN PRESCRIPTIONS on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = PRESCRIPTIONS.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."DAYS_STAY" > "23" AND PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "Phenylephrine HCl"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'prescriptions'], 'cond': [['demographic."days_stay"', '>', '23'], ['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'phenylephrine hcl']]}
----------
TRUTH:
SELECT PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG_DOSE" FROM PRESCRIPTIONS WHERE PRESCRIPTIONS."DRUG" = "Phenytoin"
{'sel': '', 'agg': ['prescriptions."drug_dose"'], 'tab': ['prescriptions'], 'cond': [['prescriptions."drug"', '=', 'phenytoin']]}

how many patients are married and diagnosed with primary disease coronary artery disease or coronary artery bypass graft with mvr maze?
----------
PREDS:
SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."MARITAL_STATUS" = "MARRIED" AND DEMOGRAPHIC."DIAGNOSIS" = "CORONARY ARTERY DISEASE\CORONARY ARTERY BYPASS GRAFT; WITH MVR;MAZE/SDA"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic'], 'cond': [['demographic."diagnosis"', '=', 'coronary artery disease\\coronary artery bypass graft; with mvr;maze/sda'], ['demographic."marital_status"', '=', 'married']]}
----------

SELECT COUNT ( DISTINCT DEMOGRAPHIC."SUBJECT_ID" ) FROM DEMOGRAPHIC INNER JOIN LAB on DEMOGRAPHIC.HADM_ID = LAB.HADM_ID WHERE DEMOGRAPHIC."INSURANCE" = "Medicare" AND LAB."LABEL" = "Amylase"
{'sel': 'count', 'agg': ['demographic."subject_id"'], 'tab': ['demographic', 'lab'], 'cond': [['demographic."insurance"', '=', 'medicare'], ['lab."label"', '=', 'amylase']]}

TRUTH:
